## 24. Sources and reference register

Sources were consulted across the preceding revisions on 22 September 2026. R42–R51 support Cloudflare-first design; R52–R71 support revision 3. Revision 4 reread the original briefs [R72–R73] and rechecked selected primary API/eligibility/signing/privacy references, including R10, R39, R42, R43 and R64. R74 remains an unreadable input rather than a source of invented requirements. R75–R77 restore source/reference continuity and typed-decision semantics. Existing references retain their historical inspection status; not every inherited source was reread in this preparation. Recheck mutable policies, SDKs and deployments before release; a URL or source label is not operational proof. Repository README descriptions establish proposed project roles, not production reliability or full security review. The prior audit is historical evidence. User-provided footage and images are visual requirements. Current API, entitlement, package and licensing details must be rechecked at implementation/qualification time.

The source inventory and reviewed files are read-only evidence; this document does not claim that any listed library was installed, the full application was tested, production data was inspected, or a release was pushed in this work.

## 25. Owner-request coverage and use-case traceability

### 25.1 Coverage is a design claim, not a software pass

The following **44 owner-commitment rows** reconcile the expressed requests across this conversation and the recovered original briefs. Every row is covered by this document and has implementation and proof anchors. None is marked product-VERIFIED merely because it appears here. The source-input limitations in section 2.5 remain explicit; unseen Claude-artifact or missing-skill contents cannot be certified from inference.

Task numbers in the compact table below mean `TASK-NNN`; requirement IDs keep their full form. Golden journey names are defined in section 26. The existing detailed acceptance of each use case still applies; a journey is an additional integrated check, not a replacement for its negative tests.

### 25.3 Conflict-resolution ledger

| Potential conflict | Binding resolution |
|---|---|
| “Absolute truth” versus generated/inferred knowledge | Authoritative source records, provenance, human-verification limits and corrections; graph/model output is not self-certifying truth. |
| “Instant” versus permission and incomplete speech | Fast acknowledgment/preparation; commit only a stable authorized intent. Keep latency boundaries and slow/failure cases visible. |
| “Cloudflare retains nothing” versus operational visibility | No protected content persistence in the qualified path; explicitly minimized authorized metadata is retained for a defined period. No-training and no-content-retention are separate. |
| Shared knowledge versus encrypted device-only records | Approved storage/sharing/key-management path, never silent plaintext publication or uploaded device master key. The existing wiki plaintext boundary must be visible. |
| “Local optional by device” versus automatic model installs | Hardware recommends eligibility; user or trusted explicit policy authorizes installation and selection separately. |
| “Laya free” versus deployment and other models | No Jev API fee is not zero serving/speech/generation cost or automatic feature equivalence. Bring up Laya without a Jev key. |
| “PCC default” versus public native DMG | Eligible native generative workloads only, actual entitlement and authorized distribution checked. Cloudflare remains the speech default. |
| “Always join” versus lobby, organizer removal, platform rules and privacy | Automatically attempt only opted-in eligible occurrences using supported real events; respect admission/removal, never stealth rejoin or silent fallback capture. |
| “Teams makes it GDPR compliant” | Teams is a user interface/participant route; lawful basis, notices, contracts, rights and approved media procedures remain separate evidence gates. |
| “Fewer lines” versus safe maintainable behavior | Characterize and simplify responsibility boundaries; preserve validation, security, accessibility, diagnostics and tests. |
| “Test then sign” versus immutable evidence | Sign/timestamp before final artifact acceptance; publish exactly the tested bytes. Rebuild or re-sign creates a new candidate. |
| “One document” versus low token overhead | One canonical specification, section-scoped retrieval, generated compact task/coverage artifacts and a minimal always-loaded invariant set. |

## 26. Golden end-to-end proof journeys

These **12 integrated proof journeys** close the gaps between component tests. They do not add 12 unrelated features or replace the 112 individual use cases. Every run records its exact source/artifact/service/profile identity, native hardware where needed, selected real providers, expected postconditions and failure denominator. Use synthetic or explicitly authorized data and label mock-only work separately. An uncut video supplements—not replaces—machine-readable and canonical result evidence.

### FLOW-01 — A new Windows user reaches the real first success

**Coverage:** UC-001–UC-003; UC-009–UC-014; UC-049–UC-050; UC-057; UC-065–UC-069. **Primary tasks:** 002 006 016 019 027 030 031 033 034 045 056 063 064.

**Run:** Install the final identified signed cloud-first core on a clean supported Windows profile. Verify signer/bytes, sign in to the authorized Entra tenant, fetch effective policy and confirm no optional local models or organizational provider tokens were delivered. With real permission, say “Hey Métis, open Google”; observe the bar, actual Cloudflare caption, configured Jev decision where needed, authorized dispatch and verified target. Close the explicitly resolved safe target gracefully. Reconcile its metadata in the actual portal, then stop capture and inspect privacy sinks.

**Negative cases and proof:** Wrong tenant, denied microphone, unavailable provider, expired policy, stale renderer override and unsaved-work close must produce accurate states without bypass or surprise fallback. Record first-value waterfall, actual provider/attempt identity, native timings, final artifact hash and portal/schema revision. No fixture row or prerecorded action is accepted.

### FLOW-02 — The complete reference-video workflow and visual experience

**Coverage:** UC-010–UC-025; UC-026; UC-040. **Primary tasks:** 027 028 029 030 033 056 065.

**Run:** On the real native Mac app, execute Notes → create one note titled “hello” → Arc → Google search “Norbert Wiener” → X.com → Photo Booth capture under explicit current authorization. Resolve each next reference from the preceding verified object. Repeat the supported declared equivalents on Windows. Keep the actual solving orb inside the animated bar and live caption above, with required permission/confirmation feedback visible.

**Negative cases and proof:** Verify note title/content/object, intended browser and query, no X posting and one local photo. Missing app, camera busy/denied, late cancellation, changed target, manual interference and repeated final callbacks cannot fabricate success. A second intentional note request can create a second note. Record uncut actual motion/interaction; sign-in screens/private content are not published.

### FLOW-03 — Dual decision providers and one-key fleet isolation

**Coverage:** UC-004–UC-008; UC-015–UC-016; UC-048; UC-049; UC-052; UC-055; UC-069. **Primary tasks:** 012 015 019 031 032 033 034 040 056.

**Run:** Two independently enrolled devices use one server-held Jev key with separate user data. Prove an applied command decision and a useful Intelligence assessment. Test atomic key rotation and failure of a replacement without exposing either key. Then run the same qualified candidate contract through the actual Laya service with no Jev key available to that route, including an Intelligence classification. Account separately for each serving path.

**Negative cases and proof:** Cross-user context, forged identity, revoked seat, incompatible operation/target heads, stale snapshot, ambiguous utterance, provider overload and Laya-only fallback are negative cases. Remote/meeting voices cannot authorize a local action. A rejected old generation cannot execute after Stop. Keep deterministic availability distinct from a working model call.

### FLOW-04 — A long, faithful meeting with bounded private working memory

**Coverage:** UC-033–UC-039; UC-066–UC-069; UC-075–UC-076. **Primary tasks:** 013 015 016 017 018 020 034 053 054 057.

**Run:** Run a permitted two-hour representative meeting with only selected source tracks, supported language/code-switch slices and realistic names, amounts and negations. Observe real interim replacements, finals, gaps and speaker uncertainty. Produce only policy-permitted approved summaries/actions and valid source references. Stop normally and verify terminal/tail behavior; repeat revocation/stop-all without continuing unauthorized submission.

**Negative cases and proof:** Exercise echo, overlap, sample-rate mismatch, headset replacement, clock drift, CPU/memory pressure, sleep/network reconnect and silent/no-speech input. Compare held-out human reference and critical-error metrics; no fluent rewrite or fabricated recovery of missing audio. Inspect transcript/display/summary memory limits and all controlled persistence, including error paths. Separate wall-clock duration, track duration and billed attempts.

### FLOW-05 — Intelligence, Dust and corrected source truth converge

**Coverage:** UC-041–UC-048; UC-077–UC-090; UC-112. **Primary tasks:** 006 007 031 032 035 036 037 038 039 040 041 054 058.

**Run:** Save approved meeting knowledge under the actual canonical authority. Ask where the account/project stands; display deterministic facts, separate Jev/Laya assessments, evidence dates and allowed explanation. Retrieve it through real Dust personal/scoped identity while the desktop sleeps. Commit an allowed action, propose a protected-field edit, resolve a human correction, regenerate the wiki/graph and read the final revision from both surfaces.

**Negative cases and proof:** Concurrent writes, lost publication event, stale search watermark, similar names, mixed currencies, deadline crossing without edits, source denial/deletion and graph degree/count leakage must be tested. Verify after canonical commit rather than retry a possibly completed write. Revoke access and restore a stale backup/device; no deleted or unauthorized knowledge may reappear. Dust retention/replica limitations are explicit.

### FLOW-06 — Central skills change without reinstalling Métis

**Coverage:** UC-028–UC-030; UC-050–UC-056; UC-091–UC-096. **Primary tasks:** 009 037 042 043 044 045 055.

**Run:** An authorized author creates a declarative skill with typed inputs, bounded evidence/tools and budgets; evaluation/review publishes immutable v1. An entitled installed client discovers it and executes a real contextual request on the approved service. Publish v2 during a run: the active run remains pinned, a later run uses v2. Revoke a cached version and prove denial. Verify actual per-skill/version/provider attempt metadata and results.

**Negative cases and proof:** Reject executable uploads, embedded secret/customer examples, scope escalation, excessive fan-out and unsupported tools. A malicious knowledge snippet cannot become skill instructions. Cancel during a remote step and reconcile existing side effects. Role-limited author/reviewer/support users cannot promote their own unauthorized version or view private inputs through analytics.

### FLOW-07 — Simple Settings and optional local models work on real hardware

**Coverage:** UC-007; UC-058–UC-059; UC-062; UC-065; UC-070–UC-074; UC-097–UC-100. **Primary tasks:** 008 021 022 024 025 027 044 059 060.

**Run:** A nontechnical user finds microphone/privacy/attendance controls in the four destinations, previews effective policy and changes a permitted setting. On actual supported hardware, explicitly request a recommended local speech pack, see bytes and progress, verify/self-test it, select local-only, use it, then disable/unload/remove it. Local generation and vision remain independent and absent unless separately selected.

**Negative cases and proof:** Test old-key migration, failed save, a policy update mid-form, unsupported language/accelerator, low memory/disk, metered/captive network, interrupted transfer and heat/power limits. No hardware-based download consent, silent selection or cloud fallback. Model download is disclosed network access; local-only speech itself has no cloud audio egress. Measure user-task success and no competing capture starvation.

### FLOW-08 — Visible Teams assistance survives lifecycle and audience changes

**Coverage:** UC-100–UC-111; UC-077; UC-085; UC-087; UC-093. **Primary tasks:** 006 010 046 047 048 049 050 051 052 055 058.

**Run:** Install the authenticated Teams app and enable eligible automatic attendance. A real supported occurrence-start signal causes one named assistant to request entry, receive admission and satisfy actual media/status prerequisites before processing or persisting derived data as permitted. With the desktop offline, stream allowed media to Cloudflare, create approved knowledge, let Dust perform an allowed update, execute a scoped skill and show the corresponding Operator receipt.

**Negative cases and proof:** Test recurrence/reschedule/cancel, duplicate enrolled colleagues, late installation, lobby denial, organizer removal, manual skip, no-host timeout and remote Stop during a partition. Exactly one authorized source is intended; a stale lease cannot capture or rejoin. Remote utterances never operate an attendee desktop. External attendees cannot view private sources. Qualify Zoom/Meet independently; missing program/media eligibility remains BLOCKED, not replaced by stealth browser capture or a mislabeled post-meeting import.

### FLOW-09 — Install, migrate, update and recover the signed artifact

**Coverage:** UC-057–UC-064; UC-098. **Primary tasks:** 023 024 026 027 062 063 064 065.

**Run:** Install and upgrade from the actual supported public 1.x distribution using the qualified core and compatible backend. Preserve identity, notes, encryption keys, explicit offline choices, permissions and selected storage. Download one authorized optional component during onboarding, verify it and activate it atomically. Publish the already tested signed Windows artifact and retrieve it from the canonical feed.

**Negative cases and proof:** Probe bad/expired manifest, signer/hash mismatch, architecture mismatch, path traversal/decompression abuse, network cut, low disk, reboot and interrupted updater. Never activate partial bytes or mutate a signed Mac app bundle. Do not roll back onto a prohibited vulnerable component. Installed/new/old backend compatibility and non-admin/managed deployment must be proven; Apple public-feed state remains separate.

### FLOW-10 — No hidden content persistence or cross-boundary authority

**Coverage:** UC-004–UC-008; UC-024; UC-032; UC-060; UC-067–UC-069; UC-072; UC-076; UC-082–UC-083; UC-086; UC-090; UC-094–UC-095; UC-103; UC-106–UC-108; UC-112. **Primary tasks:** 011 013 015 033 041 049 050 057 058 062.

**Run:** Run synthetic content/secret sentinels through success, refusal, errors, retries, cancellation, import, decision, skills, graph generation and media reception. Read back actual deployed privacy settings and inspect every controlled configured log/cache/storage/export sink. Confirm the approved metadata ledger still works. Test roles, audiences, redirects, helper authority, script injection, malicious documents and revoked identities.

**Negative cases and proof:** Cloudflare flags alone do not attest to Dust/Microsoft/model-provider retention. Document supplier boundaries and unresolved approvals. A plaintext wiki/embedding is content; no relabelling loophole. Rights tests cover source/projection/replica and restoration. Application-managed memory lifetime is bounded without claiming physical secure erasure of OS swap or uninspectable infrastructure.

### FLOW-11 — Native Mac parity and Apple routes without a false public release

**Coverage:** UC-001–UC-003; UC-009–UC-016; UC-033–UC-040; UC-048; UC-053; UC-063; UC-065; UC-070–UC-074. **Primary tasks:** 029 030 033 034 056 059 063 065.

**Run:** Build the actual Swift/SwiftUI application through Xcode and run the promised native window/audio/control/knowledge journeys. Qualify native orb/beam assets and permissions. Cloudflare remains speech default; explicitly qualify optional OS-managed speech/generation. Where PCC is truly eligible, test supported SDK requests, quota/readiness and honest portal metadata; otherwise report the precise route limitation.

**Negative cases and proof:** Exercise older/unsupported device or OS, disabled Apple Intelligence, entitlement absence, quota exhaustion, unavailable model assets and data/keychain migration. A public Developer ID DMG cannot advertise PCC eligibility without actual authorization. No invented Codex 2 flag, universal free gateway, unsigned public DMG or whole-app WebView approximation. Windows evidence can close independently of Apple publication inputs.

### FLOW-12 — An operator and the next coding session can trust the system

**Coverage:** UC-041–UC-048; UC-049–UC-056; UC-064; UC-075; UC-096; UC-099. **Primary tasks:** 001 003 004 034 040 045 060 061 062 063 066.

**Run:** Use a representative authorized fleet/concurrency envelope to reconcile real people/devices/bots/sessions and provider quantities, test 2,001-record totals and late/cumulative/retry cases, and observe data-health degradation. Exercise provider/key/database/knowledge outage, restore, canary and kill-switch procedures. After an approved refactor, another Codex session resumes using the correct worktree map, task state, lessons and evidence.

**Negative cases and proof:** No live-user proxy becomes employee-performance monitoring, no missing usage becomes zero and no soft threshold becomes a hard-cap claim. Stale branch/dirty file/renamed symbol invalidates engineering context. Count graph/index/reading overhead against competent scoped search. Every release assertion resolves to a fresh evidence receipt; superseded source, changed policy or different signed bytes invalidate applicable old passes.

## 27. Machine-checkable evidence and release decision

### 27.1 Build the acceptance registry from the contract

In TASK-002, create a compact versioned repository registry from the stable IDs in sections 8, 20, 21 and 25. It includes every requirement, every individual use case, all owner coverage rows, tasks/slices, applicable platform/profile, test identifier, evidence level, dependencies, authorized scope decision and current status. Generate task views rather than maintaining contradictory hand-written counts. Keep the single master document authoritative until an owner-reviewed change revises it.

The following is an **unexecuted example record**. Values marked null must be filled from actual results; a timestamp, file link or success state must not be fabricated to satisfy a schema.

```json
{
  "schemaVersion": "metis.acceptance.v1",
  "requirementIds": ["M2-E2E-01", "M2-WIN-01"],
  "useCaseIds": ["UC-001", "UC-009", "UC-057"],
  "taskSlice": "TASK-063.WIN",
  "flowId": "FLOW-01",
  "platform": "windows",
  "profile": "cloudflare-first",
  "state": "NOT_TESTED",
  "evidenceLevel": "native-signed-and-deployed",
  "sourceCommit": null,
  "artifactSha256": null,
  "signerIdentity": null,
  "timestampVerification": null,
  "backendDeployment": null,
  "schemaRevision": null,
  "policyRevision": null,
  "componentManifestRevision": null,
  "modelAndConfigurationRevision": null,
  "datasetAndScoringRevision": null,
  "commandsAndExitCodes": [],
  "permittedEvidenceLocations": [],
  "executedAt": null,
  "reviewer": null,
  "limitations": ["Example only; no product test has run."]
}
```

Event/run identifiers must be opaque and validated. Metadata fields such as model, task and status are constrained against trusted registries, not free-form strings that can carry a prompt. Detailed permitted evidence lives in the authorized test/knowledge environment; do not copy private input/output into Operator merely because the evidence schema contains a reference field.

### 27.2 Structural checks to implement and run

The contract linter must check all of the following before a plan or release report claims complete coverage:

1. All **55 established requirement IDs**, **112 unique catalog use-case IDs** and **66 unique root task IDs** exist. Preserve the previous IDs and mappings; new requirements need an explicit owner-reviewed diff.
2. Every owner commitment has requirement, task and proof anchors. Every individual use case has an implementation and a matching actual test definition. No range is treated as one passing test.
3. Every referenced requirement, task, suffix slice, source and flow exists. Common implementation dependencies and scope-specific integration gates resolve to an acyclic graph with valid outputs. Distinguish an adapter contract from its later end-to-end acceptance to avoid circular prerequisites.
4. Every applicable advertised requirement has evidence at its required level. IMPLEMENTED, MOCKED, NOT_TESTED, BLOCKED, skipped and unsupported are not VERIFIED. Invalid/missing artifact or deployment identity rejects a release-level receipt.
5. Feature flags, failed tests, disabled integrations and unresolved external prerequisites cannot disappear from the final denominator. A narrower release requires explicit owner scope approval and truthful release notes.
6. Final Windows acceptance references the signed artifact hash after all required signing/timestamping. Published/downloaded hash must match. A changed byte, helper, manifest or relevant backend/policy invalidates affected old evidence.
7. All performance/quality claims name measurement boundary, sample size, hardware/network, dataset/scoring, errors/abstentions and proposed budget or qualified result. A source-regex test or simulated view cannot satisfy a native or live-service claim.
8. Sources and external prerequisites are not automatically refreshed by a passing code test. Track review date, applicable version, assurance owner and conditions that require requalification.
9. The original 20 historical findings have current VERIFIED, NOT_REPRODUCED_WITH_EVIDENCE, BLOCKED or owner-approved disposition. Neither an old report nor a branch title proves present status.
10. The short Codex launch text references the same full scope as the registry. The document's former UC-076 cutoff must not return through a stale template.

These are checks on traceability and receipts, not an algorithm that can certify legal compliance or semantic truth. A real reviewer still verifies whether the cited evidence supports the claim. Codex must never self-approve a permission expansion, legal basis or publisher identity.

### 27.3 Lifecycle states and stale evidence

Use separate fields for implementation state, deployment state, verification result and applicability. A single status string such as “done” hides too much. A source change can preserve a still-valid independent component result only through an explicit impact review; final artifact acceptance always binds the actual tested artifact. A newer provider alias, rubric, policy, source ACL or DB migration may invalidate relevant evidence even if desktop code did not change.

Track a parent task as PARTIAL when some required slices pass and others do not. Keep a passed .WIN result usable without waiting on .MAC public credentials. Keep a failed privacy/authorization gate blocking every dependent content-processing claim. A mocked .CONTRACT slice permits independent development but never unlocks real customer content or public promotion.

### 27.4 The release decision algorithm

For each proposed platform/profile release, resolve its owner-approved advertised scope. Expand the required shared controls, exact capabilities, use cases and service dependencies. Require actual compatible evidence for each applicable row and a reviewed disposition for every excluded/blocked row. Reject unresolved critical security/privacy failures, missing mandatory native/quality/operation evidence, inaccurate labels or a changed final artifact.

For Windows, require legitimate signer/timestamp and verified post-upload bytes. For native Mac public distribution, require its actual Apple route prerequisites in addition to native functional evidence. For Teams/live media, require its actual permission/status/hosting/privacy prerequisites; a personal tab or imported transcript does not satisfy them. Require meaningful recovery and the actual deployed backend, not only source configuration.

Publish only the frozen approved artifact. Mark the whole Métis 2.0 contract complete only when all promised required scopes are satisfied or the owner has explicitly revised the contract; a scoped release and full-contract completion are different decisions.

### 27.5 Final human-readable report

Deliver one concise summary containing: changes and existing paths reused; source and deployed identities; the scope actually shipped; all 112 case statuses by relevant platform/profile; the 55 requirement dispositions; golden-flow receipts; transcript and critical-error scores; measured latency/size/resource improvement; actual Jev/Laya and Intelligence results; canonical/Dust/skill correctness; Teams/privacy/rights evidence; signature/hash/download evidence; recovery results; and the smallest unresolved blockers with their owners and next operations.

Include a limited residual-risk register instead of an unexplained readiness score. Specifically name inaccessible source inputs, supplier/tenant approvals, unsupported hardware/platform conditions, uncertainty in transcript/knowledge quality, retention limits outside controlled sinks and missing final native evidence. Explain which features still work under failure without weakening policy. The next Codex session must be able to resume from CURRENT.md and the actual working tree without guessing what was done.

### 27.6 What was validated while preparing this revision

The preparation script checks preservation and uniqueness of the 55 requirement IDs, 112 catalog rows and 66 task definitions; coverage of all requirements by owner commitments; exhaustive, non-overlapping use-case ownership ranges; valid task/requirement/flow references; the acyclic common-prerequisite graph; retention of all 20 historical findings; and correction of the stale launch instruction and signing order. The PDF is rendered and inspected separately for layout/text completeness.

This is **document validation only**. No deployed task graph, production database, application test suite, microphone, Jev/Laya service, Teams meeting, Dust write, native installer or signing operation has been executed by this revision's preparation. Codex must produce and validate the real evidence, including the detailed scope-specific graph and test definitions, before closing product gates.


---

**Final acceptance principle:** a verified user, under the effective policy, asks for a supported action; the correct backend helps only where needed; trusted code performs and verifies the right result; the transcript remains faithful; cloud content persistence is prevented under the qualified route; local models remain explicit and device-qualified; consumption is attributable; the application stays responsive; installation is proportionate; and the actual released binary can be trusted. Shared knowledge remains source-backed and permissioned; Dust changes are verified canonical updates; skills are centrally governed; meeting participation is visible, supported and authorized. Anything less remains a visible engineering task, not a marketing claim.


## 28. Complete meeting experience and competitive qualification

Competition is an outcome target, not an official certification or affiliation. The requested comparison set is Cluely, Plaud, Demodesk and Granola. Their publicly described workflows informed these qualifications; no hands-on head-to-head result is claimed. See R80–R85. Do not rebuild another product or add every competitor feature. Preserve Métis’s distinctive combination: visible trusted assistance, verified actions, source-backed shared knowledge and centrally governed skills.

The twelve EXP contracts below are mandatory refinements of existing capabilities. Keep all baseline M2/UC/TASK IDs; record separate untested EXP receipts and source findings. Each EXP must have individual actual test definitions and applicable platform/service slices before closing. A group-level screenshot is not twelve passes.

### EXP-01 — Prepared, not surprised

**Owners:** TASK-008, TASK-037, TASK-040, TASK-046, TASK-047, TASK-055. **Baseline requirements:** M2-KNOW-01, M2-SET-01, M2-TEAMS-01.

One calm Today view offers the next eligible meeting, last confirmed discussion, outstanding commitments, key unknowns and source dates. Pre-meeting briefs use only authorized context; private-to-me does not imply shareable-to-the-room. Absent calendar access is a clear manual route, not a blank spinner.

**Acceptance:** A user finds the relevant last commitment within two deliberate interactions on the defined fixture; wrong-account, guest and expired-source tests deny disclosure.

### EXP-02 — Human notes remain the user’s work

**Owners:** TASK-005, TASK-018, TASK-035, TASK-040, TASK-043, TASK-054. **Baseline requirements:** M2-DATA-01, M2-KNOW-02, M2-SKILL-02.

Support lightweight human-authored notes and explicit important-moment markers during a meeting. Keep raw human notes, generated enhancements and accepted corrections as distinct revisions. Enhancements are previewed and can be accepted by block; re-run never overwrites newer edits. A moment marker is not permission to retain an audio clip. Persist permitted human notes only in the chosen knowledge environment, never telemetry.

**Acceptance:** Typing while a partial transcript arrives, enhancement returns late, storage fails or another editor changes a block does not lose human text. The original and accepted revision remain explainable; unretained source intervals are not fabricated quotations.

### EXP-03 — Useful, quiet live assistance

**Owners:** TASK-017, TASK-018, TASK-030, TASK-037, TASK-043, TASK-055, TASK-056, TASK-060. **Baseline requirements:** M2-ASR-01, M2-SKILL-02, M2-UX-02.

Provide Catch me up, What should I ask?, Explain this term and commitments-so-far in a small optional in-meeting panel. Tie each answer to current authorized interval/source freshness. A coalesced foreground request outranks background analysis. Do not trigger models on every transcript token; no unsolicited speech, focus grabbing or automatic sending.

**Acceptance:** After a topic change, obsolete suggestions expire without erasing pinned human notes. Verify source intervals, catch-up coverage, request count, latency and no duplicate audio session under real capture.

### EXP-04 — Trusted follow-through, not a wall of prose

**Owners:** TASK-009, TASK-033, TASK-035, TASK-042, TASK-043, TASK-044, TASK-052, TASK-054, TASK-055. **Baseline requirements:** M2-ACT-01, M2-SKILL-01, M2-SKILL-02, M2-DATA-01.

Turn an approved summary into a short review inbox: decision, action, owner, due date, evidence and unresolved fields. Draft follow-ups and CRM updates as separate proposals. Show exact destination/object and field diff before consequential commit; resolve unknown outcomes with readback. Drafting, saving, sharing and sending are distinct permissions.

**Acceptance:** One supported CRM/task/email route is demonstrated with exact-payload approval, idempotency, stale edit conflict and remote object receipt. No universal connector claims or unauthorized automatic sync.

### EXP-05 — A powerful notebook with fewer settings

**Owners:** TASK-008, TASK-022, TASK-028, TASK-040, TASK-044, TASK-055. **Baseline requirements:** M2-SET-01, M2-SET-02, M2-UX-02.

Today, meeting workspace, search and review should be reachable without a vendor/model menu. Preserve the four Settings destinations and all old-key dispositions. Show recovery and effective policy in context. Include back-to-back meeting handover, clean empty states, narrow windows, keyboard/screen-reader flow and native conventions.

**Acceptance:** Run the existing nontechnical task study plus back-to-back and permission-denied cases; measure unassisted success, mistakes and time rather than counting screenshots.

### EXP-06 — Vocabulary and template quality without false certainty

**Owners:** TASK-009, TASK-018, TASK-037, TASK-042, TASK-043, TASK-053, TASK-054. **Baseline requirements:** M2-ASR-02, M2-SKILL-01, M2-KNOW-02.

Use reviewed, scoped vocabulary only on a transport that supports it and qualifies for privacy. It biases recognition; it cannot substitute a term against evidence. Keep all nine existing role-specific recaps and support reviewed alternate summaries from the same authorized record without overwriting original or human-accepted content.

**Acceptance:** Held-out names/amounts/negations include plausible-but-wrong glossary matches. Compare critical errors and corrections by language, not just aggregate WER; explicitly unavailable provider features do not get invented flags.

### EXP-07 — Capture choices with truthful recovery

**Owners:** TASK-005, TASK-017, TASK-018, TASK-024, TASK-025, TASK-029, TASK-049, TASK-050, TASK-051, TASK-053, TASK-054. **Baseline requirements:** M2-STT-03, M2-ASR-01, M2-TEAMS-04, M2-DATA-01.

Qualify live command, desktop meeting, supported bot/media and authorized audio/transcript import independently. Capture is neither covert nor universal. Use one occurrence source lease. Safe imports are format/size/sniff constrained, preserve timezone/speaker uncertainty and never silently upload a user-selected local-only file.

**Acceptance:** Handover from a failed source is explicit and gap-aware, not a second hidden recorder. Crash/reboot cannot resume an expired recording authority. Imported material cannot arm desktop actions or bypass a selected retention profile.

### EXP-08 — A real enterprise entitlement and trust plane

**Owners:** TASK-002, TASK-006, TASK-012, TASK-014, TASK-034, TASK-045, TASK-062, TASK-063. **Baseline requirements:** M2-SEC-01, M2-SEC-02, M2-OPS-01.

Resolve the source export’s separate legacy license and Operator capability paths. Document issuance, seat/device ownership, offline lease, authority, expiry, revocation and bootstrap. Shared funding is not shared access. Existing legitimate data access/recovery remains possible under a spending outage.

**Acceptance:** Wrong tenant, dev trust key, copied lease, concurrency race and revoked seat fail appropriate capability boundaries. No unbounded paid relay or accidental second license authority.

### EXP-09 — No silent loss or misleading success

**Owners:** TASK-005, TASK-018, TASK-029, TASK-033, TASK-035, TASK-039, TASK-041, TASK-054, TASK-062. **Baseline requirements:** M2-ACT-02, M2-DATA-01, M2-KNOW-05.

Every save, command, source handover, skill and external write exposes accepted/attempted/committed/verified/unknown accurately. Use revision-aware writes, safe reconciliation and finite retries. Stored-but-indexing is not failed; queued is not committed; a model response is not an object receipt.

**Acceptance:** Inject failures between commit, receipt and projection. No duplicate side effects, missing summaries painted complete, false native save, swallowed error or resurrected revoked/deleted record.

### EXP-10 — Animated identity, bounded cost

**Owners:** TASK-019, TASK-028, TASK-029, TASK-030, TASK-060, TASK-063. **Baseline requirements:** M2-UX-01, M2-UX-02, M2-VOICE-02.

ARMED is only an animated monochrome solving orb on a transparent host, faithful to Libraries.dev. Caption, bar and voice glow appear only with the appropriate active surface. Local animation never represents network/model activity. Honor reduced motion, explicit pause and resource policies; no desktop-wide invisible input region.

**Acceptance:** Record time-separated frames plus original algorithm/preset lineage; test phase continuity, hide/show, DPR, focus, draft retention, energy policy and real native hit-testing. Pausing the visual does not silently stop or start microphone capture.

### EXP-11 — Portability and control over knowledge

**Owners:** TASK-007, TASK-035, TASK-036, TASK-038, TASK-039, TASK-041, TASK-052, TASK-058. **Baseline requirements:** M2-KNOW-05, M2-DUST-01, M2-DUST-02, M2-GOV-02.

Provide authenticated exports of permitted approved notes/actions with schema, sources, revisions and ownership. Sharing defaults to explicit principals rather than anyone with a link. Revoke links/read paths promptly. Dust edits canonical objects, not generated markdown. Exports do not quietly add unretained transcripts or excluded voiceprints.

**Acceptance:** Round-trip permitted exports, valid subset imports, CSV/HTML injection tests, redacted fields, wrong-audience links and rights workflows. Expired/deleted links cannot reveal titles/counts or cached content.

### EXP-12 — Evidence-based competitive and operational qualification

**Owners:** TASK-001, TASK-004, TASK-053, TASK-055, TASK-056, TASK-057, TASK-060, TASK-061, TASK-062, TASK-063, TASK-066. **Baseline requirements:** M2-BASE-01, M2-OPS-03, M2-ENG-01, M2-REL-01.

Compare the same consented tasks, languages, hardware/network and privacy scope against legitimate available competitor trials. Score completion, critical factual errors, user editing burden, time-to-useful-result, accessibility, total work/cost and resource footprint. Separate vendor claims, source review, simulated UX and executed product evidence. No official affiliation, infallibility or superiority claim is inferred from feature count.

**Acceptance:** Pre-register supported slices and report failures/abstentions/uncertainty, no cherry-picked clips or changed denominators. Require final signed Windows and native Mac QA receipts, actual backend and mirror-safe release facts before a public claim.

**Anchors:** `.gitleaks.toml`. [R80]

### SRC-20 — Failed cloud inventory is not an empty account

**P0** · SOURCE_DEFECT · Owners: TASK-001, TASK-012, TASK-014, TASK-062.

**Source observation:** The script catches failure of secret list and continues with an empty string, then may replace METIS_PROXY_KEYS. The code correctly refuses when it sees an existing name, but a failed inventory cannot establish absence.

**Required change:** Any retained provisioner must fail on unavailable inventory and require explicit authority for replacement. Stop using embedded shared credentials for enterprise 2.0; archive/deprecate this path safely instead of re-running it automatically.

**Exit evidence:** Access denied/network error leaves all existing secrets unchanged. No delete-existing-secret workaround or successful empty inventory inference.

**Anchors:** `cloudflare-proxy/provision-embedded-key.mjs`. [R80]

### SRC-21 — Capture privacy copy must cover screenshots, titles, URLs and helpers too

**P0** · SOURCE_POLICY_CONFLICT · Owners: TASK-005, TASK-015, TASK-033, TASK-034, TASK-054, TASK-057, TASK-058.

**Source observation:** The product includes screen/OCR paths and the audit document calls meeting-title-derived file basenames metadata. Such labels can still reveal confidential content; audio-only logging rules are incomplete.

**Required change:** Minimize approved window/region observation, exclude credentials and unrelated apps, classify derived text as content. Replace sensitive title/URL labels in operational logs with opaque IDs and approved categorical fields; avoid raw crash/heap exports.

**Exit evidence:** Synthetic meeting title, OCR phrase, URL token and filename sentinels do not persist in prohibited sinks, while authorized knowledge records and meaningful safe diagnostics remain available.

**Anchors:** `src/main/screen-capture.ts`; `src/main/screen-preprocess.ts`; `docs/AUDIT-LOG.md`. [R80]

### SRC-22 — Preserve tailored recaps and human edits when moving skills server-side

**P1** · PRESERVE_AND_EXTEND · Owners: TASK-008, TASK-009, TASK-018, TASK-022, TASK-028, TASK-037, TASK-040, TASK-042, TASK-043, TASK-044, TASK-054.

**Source observation:** The source already defines nine mode-specific recap layouts and locked mode skills. A generic new cloud summary would regress this existing capability.

**Required change:** Migrate mode IDs and versioned templates into the central registry with compatibility. Keep original human notes, accepted corrections and generated alternatives separate. Never overwrite manual edits during enhancement, re-template or retry.

**Exit evidence:** All nine layouts render within the answer scroller; update/revoke/version behavior works without reinstall; human text/corrections remain exact after regeneration.

**Anchors:** `src/shared/mode-recap.ts`; `src/main/mode-skills.ts`; `DESIGN.md`. [R80]

### SRC-23 — Dust connector checklists do not prove canonical read/write

**P0** · IMPLEMENTATION_LIMIT · Owners: TASK-006, TASK-007, TASK-035, TASK-036, TASK-037, TASK-038, TASK-039, TASK-040, TASK-041, TASK-054, TASK-055.

**Source observation:** The source includes an unchecked real-Dust verification checklist for a plaintext wiki. That is neither executed evidence nor a canonical mutation interface; the readable projection has its own access boundary.

**Required change:** Reuse the wiki safely, but make personal/service-scoped Dust tools read and mutate canonical records with revision checks and readback. Protect private sources and output audience before retrieval, regeneration and publication.

**Exit evidence:** Actual two-principal Dust read/write, conflict, regenerate, correction, revoke, deletion and laptop-asleep sequence. No raw parent-folder indexing or edits lost on regeneration.

**Anchors:** `docs/verification/mi-5-dust-e2e.md`; `src/main/brain/publish.ts`. [R80]

### SRC-24 — Every workspace needs a real test/build/release coverage owner

**P0** · EVIDENCE_GAP · Owners: TASK-001, TASK-003, TASK-005, TASK-029, TASK-053, TASK-056, TASK-057, TASK-061, TASK-062, TASK-063, TASK-065, TASK-066.

**Source observation:** Root tests explicitly chain proxy and Operator suites. Native Swift and legacy license-server have their own projects/tests, and the export lacks essential root files. Test counts/names and historical README claims do not establish current executed coverage.

**Required change:** Build a workspace/entrypoint inventory for production, test, migrations and native helpers. Run each applicable suite on the correct runtime/OS; bind tests to actual package profile and post-sign artifact. Include generated bundles and policy/data migrations in impact analysis.

**Exit evidence:** No zero-tests pass, excluded workspace, stale generated bundle, native test seam or unsigned rebuild can satisfy release evidence. Missing source/tool/tenant states are explicit and scoped.

**Anchors:** `package.json`; `vitest.config.ts`; `native-app/MetisKit/Package.swift`; `license-server/package.json`; `.github/workflows/build.yml`. [R80]

## 30. Final architecture migration and execution checkpoints

### 30.1 One application, explicit responsibilities

The target diagram is a design contract, not a statement that all nodes already exist. Keep a thin React/Electron Windows shell and genuine native SwiftUI Mac shell, a restricted bridge, main/native command and audio owners, independent typed STT/decision/generation adapters, one effective policy and entitlement boundary, and postcondition verification. The user-facing Mantu Intelligence and Operator are different surfaces with different data access.

Draw logical contracts separately from physical stores: Operator metadata in the actual D1 binding; authorized meeting summaries/actions and human notes in the selected canonical knowledge environment; wiki/graph as rebuildable ACL-bound projections; component assets in approved R2 distribution; legacy license state only where its active profile really uses it. Add actor and data-class labels to arrows. A signed event is authenticated device telemetry, not proof of a human’s Entra identity or consent. Graph data is not inherently more authoritative than its sources.

Avoid unnecessary infrastructure: no new agent framework, vector database, second credential store, duplicate microphone, duplicated license service or local graph runtime without a demonstrated gap and reviewed decision. Retain existing useful runtime/process isolation, typed IPC, cache boundaries, source provenance, sidecar integrity and nine recap layouts.

### 30.2 Twelve ordered checkpoints inside the existing 66 tasks

1. **Identify and preserve** — TASK-001/002/003/004. Acquire the complete checkout, compare source/export, list real remotes, install/profile and deployment identities, audit all workspace entrypoints, baseline failures and signer access. Do not apply a historical main SHA or auto-execute the uploaded text.
2. **Freeze authority and migration decisions** — TASK-005/006/007/008/009/010. Resolve licensing versus identity, knowledge location, cloud/no-retention modes, settings visibility and platform/capture eligibility. Preserve all old records and explicit user choices. Narrow UI/design rules may change only through the documented latest-owner decision.
3. **Repair immediate defects** — TASK-027.A/028.A plus TASK-033’s result contract. Reproduce both P1s and the right-edge issue; characterize discarded decisions, unsupported/failed outcomes, exact text and finite retry behavior. Keep fixes separate from file moves.
4. **Bring up one private real speech route** — TASK-011–020. Server credential, reviewed gateway/readback, authenticated transport, requested tracks, actual transcription and genuine opt-in local wake. No default gateway auto-creation or account token in the app.
5. **Make the core genuinely small** — TASK-021–027.B. One capability manifest governs build, install, runtime readiness and optional model catalog. Update permission copy, UI, scripts, gates, notices and migration together. No hidden first-run model transfer.
6. **Connect animated identity and exact actions** — TASK-028–033 in independent Windows/Mac slices. Use real solving motion, actual native hit regions, target-aware approvals, graceful close and verified note/photo results. Qualify Jev and Laya independently without paid-service deadlocks.
7. **Fix numbers and availability** — TASK-034/045.CORE. Durable complete-window accounting, server usage precedence, bounded quotas and readiness labels. Verify actual deployment identity and legacy entitlement migration before fleet expansion.
8. **Make meeting knowledge useful** — TASK-035–041. Preserve human notes; complete sources, corrections, always-on scoped access, wiki/graph, Dust writes, deletion and evidence-first briefings. No hidden transcript archive to compensate for missing context.
9. **Finish in-meeting help and follow-through** — TASK-042–046. Versioned central skills, calm live assist, nine role summaries, reviewed CRM/task/draft actions, receipts and accessible Teams/desktop surfaces. No automatic employee scoring or unauthorized external send.
10. **Qualify real meeting participation** — TASK-047–052. Supported start signals, one occurrence owner, admission, recording/media prerequisites, laptop-off service and correct output audience. Unsupported platforms remain visibly blocked, not disguised as universal browser automation.
11. **Measure and refactor behind evidence** — TASK-053–062. Transcript/critical-fact and EXP tests, long meeting stress, proxy/captive network, sleep/lock and data-failure tests; supplied refactor skill; safe diagnostics, source graph, endpoint hardening, complete workspace CI, threat review, backup/restore and staged operational rollout.
12. **Sign, freeze, qualify, promote and reconcile** — TASK-063–066. Legitimate Windows signer before final tests; exact immutable artifacts, canonical update feed and real mirror authorities; verified post-upload download. Mac QA proceeds, while public Apple signing/eligibility remains separately gated. The final report includes every baseline, EXP and SRC disposition.

These checkpoints are not new root task IDs and do not impose a linear wait on independent providers/platforms. One integrator owns shared schemas, release metadata and migration. Product scope is not dropped just to satisfy a checkbox.

### 30.3 Often-missed enterprise behaviors

**Network and long sessions:** qualify authenticated proxies/PAC, TLS inspection under actual organizational policy, WebSocket disconnects, captive portals, sleep and credential renewal during a long call. Never disable TLS verification. Reauthentication cannot replay stale commands. A budget block stops new spending according to policy but preserves already approved local notes and truthful partial transcripts. Test service-region routes and rate limits with measured concurrency; historical vendor limits are not an SLA.

**Workspace lifetime:** sign-out, account switch, device lock and app quit invalidate capture/command generations, privacy-relevant caches and active approvals. Deactivation is distinct from deleting user data. A shared computer cannot show another user’s captions or restore their pending write. Crash recovery obeys the active retention policy instead of silently journaling raw transcripts.

**Support and rollout:** a content-free support bundle, bounded diagnostics, documented ownership and severity response, canary/rollback, source/dependency inventory and reproducible native evidence are release requirements. Scan every workspace and release artifact; a historical security audit, permissive fixture ignore rule or a healthy homepage is not current assurance. Use real load and usability tests, not synthetic dashboards as performance evidence.

**End of lifecycle:** authorized export, correction, revoke, uninstall, optional-pack removal, key retirement and disconnected service behavior must remain usable. Uninstall never silently deletes the selected external knowledge space. A stopped update feed or a pending Apple artifact does not get silently substituted with unsigned bytes.

### 30.4 Document and receipt drift prevention

Synchronize this canonical Markdown, generated PDF/HTML, task files, registry, visual state map and launch text. The older documents and mockups remain historical references only. A package verifier checks the 55 baseline requirements, 112 use cases, 66 root tasks, 44 commitments, 12 golden flows and 20 historical findings plus the 12 EXP contracts and 24 SRC findings. Every added item has existing-task ownership, explicit test definition and NOT_TESTED status until actual evidence is supplied.

A passing handoff validator proves structure, not semantics, deployment or best-in-class quality. A live benchmark must state sample, version, scope, environment, privacy basis, errors, abstentions and limits. New source/deployment/model/policy/ACL changes invalidate affected receipts. Only actual immutable signed-artifact and service evidence closes product release gates.
